<span class="line"><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-light-font-weight:bold;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-dark-font-weight:bold">## 1. Where we are (measured + modeled)</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">Circus, E4B Q4_0, single prompt, 64 tokens, temp 0, serial:</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
